"9,000 pounds. Anderson should've mentioned it. That's my bottom line." Ukfa looked at Death Knell in Gehrman Sparrow's hands and said, "This is already cheap enough. If I weren't worried that getting to know more Beyonders would affect my present life, I would've actively promoted it. I'm sure I can sell it for 12,000 pounds."

Indeed, a mystical item at this level can be sold for sky-high prices if the negative effects aren't too serious when meeting a suitable buyer... Normally speaking, 10,000 to 12,000 pounds is a reasonable price... Although Klein had the intention of bargaining, the price was already low enough that he felt embarrassed to try and take advantage of him. With a terse acknowledgment, he said, "I'll give it a run. I'll complete the deal if there aren't any problems."

Of course, he didn't really try the gun, as it would give him a weakness for nothing. His method for inspection was to use his Spirituality to probe the gun, along with testing for the credibility of Ukfa's words via divination. He did it rather openly, without minding Ukfa's and Anderson's gazes.

I'll make confirmation later above the gray fog... However, Ukfa likely doesn't dare to lie to me. He's definitely afraid of a crazy adventurer seeking revenge on him. After all, he has already obtained a settled and quiet life. He has a wife and children... Klein placed Death Knell on the table, lifted his suitcase, and took out 9,000 pounds from it. The money had been removed from above the gray fog to "air" it ahead of time.

After Ukfa received the money, he did a quick count and confirmed the authenticity of the money.

"As expected of a recently famous adventurer. Few people can produce 9,000 pounds in cash at once. Even a tycoon doesn't have that much liquidity," he said ruefully as he put away the stacks of cash.

I even spent 5,000 pounds to buy a key not long ago... Klein felt his heart suddenly turn empty as he saw the 9,000 pounds enter Ukfa's drawer.

I've been out at sea for so long, saving up so much money, but in just moments, it's all gone... Now, I'm left with 2,683 pounds and 6 gold coins. I can't even afford a relatively decent manor... Klein sighed as he took out the ordinary revolver from his underarm holster, removed the bullets inside, and stuffed them into Death Knell.

After Anderson watched the entire transaction, he said with a tsk, "Ukfa, you've changed. In the past, you would've checked the authenticity of each note. If you find it troublesome, I can help you!"

"It's totally fine, but I'm worried that Gehrman will shoot you." Ukfa clearly knew the Strongest Hunter's talent at provocation.

He could imagine the scene of Anderson posing in a way that was begging to be beaten--counting each and every note while verifying them against the light in slow motion.

Well said! Klein silently praised him. He then stuffed the iron black, long-barreled revolver into his underarm holster.

The original revolver was thrown inside the suitcase.

"Thanks to you, I no longer have to fret over this matter," Ukfa said with a smile as he pointed at the door. "I'll get my valet to send you out."

Anderson opened his mouth as he chuckled.

"Ukfa, aren't you going to keep us here for dinner?"

"When you're married with kids, I'll treat you to the best food at the best restaurant," Ukfa said with a smile, completely unfazed by his accusation.

Outside the manor, Anderson looked up with narrowed eyes. At the sight of the setting sun, he chuckled.

"Back when I knew Ukfa, he was a doctor who was good at planting all kinds of strange plants on the ship to improve everyone's lives. I thought he would die early on during our adventures, but to my surprise, he was always lucky. He even became a Druid later."

Why are your wistful words also so deserving of a beating... Klein deliberately said, "He does have good luck.

"As your companion, luck is needed to survive."

Anderson turned his head in surprise as he scrutinized Gehrman Sparrow.

"You know how to mock others? Or have you been infected by me?"

He wasn't too affected by it as he straightened his clothes, took off his hat, and said with a smile, "Alright, you've gotten the mystical item you need. If there's nothing else, it's time I embark on my own journey."

"Don't forget that demigod's mission." Klein succeeded in using a single sentence to crumple Anderson's expression.

"I already have an urge that compels me to finish that matter as soon as possible. Alright, there's no need for goodbyes. Perhaps we might really meet again one day." Anderson gave a self-deprecating laugh, waved the hat in his hand, and turned to head towards another path leading to Bayam.

As he watched the Strongest Hunter depart, Klein slowly heaved a sigh. Holding his suitcase, he followed the path he took to get here and headed for Bayam beneath the red evening sky and the shelter of the distinctive palm trees.

Inside Bayam City.

After finding an ordinary inn to stay in, Klein began considering his subsequent plans.

I'm finally free. I can attempt to act as a Marionettist. I have to figure out the corresponding principles. That's the most pressing issue for now.

Yes, Marionettist. The focus should be on the marionette. I haven't actually created a marionette and controlled it in battle. I should start from there.

This matter can be completed before returning to Backlund. Not only do Beyonders have to stay hidden there, making it difficult to encounter one, but no matter what one does, it's easy to get on the bad side of the Church or some important figure. I need to be careful. It's not a good place to select and create marionettes. The sea is better. I'll go make my rounds at the bar later. I'll find a pirate who deserves death to give it a try.

With this in mind, Klein immediately got up and left his room. As though he was bringing his bank certificate to a bank to withdraw the money, he headed for the nearby Seaweed Bar. Famous pirates often showed up there.

Soon, he arrived at the bar's entrance. After straightening his clothes, he pushed open the heavy wooden door.

Gazes subconsciously swept past his face before they moved away as though it wasn't anything extraordinary. Following that, someone shouted with a suppressed voice, "Gehrman Sparrow!"

In a blink of an eye, several figures in the bar ran for the back door. Before Klein could figure out what was happening, the bar was left nearly deserted.

Chapter 709: Elland's Warning

As Klein looked at the bar, which was so silent that he could hear breathing, he was taken aback. Only then did he step in and walk straight to the bar counter as though nothing had happened.

I should change my appearance the next time I come... he thought in frustration, just short of facepalming.

Ukfa's lack of awareness that Gehrman Sparrow had successfully hunted Slaughterer Kircheis had made Klein believe that the latest news hadn't reached the City of Generosity, and he boldly came to the Seaweed Bar. To his surprise, the problem lay in Ukfa who had kept a distance from the adventurer circles. He was a little behind on the news, and the crazy adventurer's infamy had already spread amongst the pirates around the Rorsted Archipelago.

Sighing silently, Klein sat in front of the bar counter and rapped the wooden counter.

"One glass of Southville beer."

"Six pence." The bartender swallowed with great difficulty.

Klein took out a few copper pence and placed them in front of him. Without a change in expression, he asked, "Any developments recently?"

The bartender took the money and carefully delivered the beer to Gehrman Sparrow. Then, he forced a smile and said, "Admiral Amyrius Rieveldt has been transferred back to Backlund. Admiral Robert Davis has replaced him, becoming the highest-ranking naval commander of the surrounding waters for the kingdom. The archipelago's situation is a little tense, and many pirate crews have sent people to gather intelligence."

Admiral Amyrius was ultimately implicated by his brother and lost his post... However, as long as he wasn't directly involved, it can't get worse for him when considering his status as a demigod. At the very least, he can maintain the treatment he receives as an admiral. Nothing will happen to his family either. Once the matter blows over, he might still have a chance of being a highranking member of the navy... Klein gulped down the beer and asked in passing, "Which family is Robert Davis from?"

"No, he's not a noble. He's one of the rare officers who slowly rose through the ranks to become an admiral. In the Berserk Sea, Sonia Island, and East Balam, he has rendered plenty of meritorious services." The bartender recalled the commendations the papers recently had for Admiral Robert Davis. After a pause, he said, "However, I heard that he has received the sponsorship of many nobles."

Yes, with the Imperial Navy, admirals who are from the royal family or aristocrats, or have close ties with them take up at least 80%. The remaining 20% are mainly rear admirals and vice admirals... Klein had long learned of the situation from the documents he received from Amyrius Rieveldt.

Compared to this, the army was a lot better.

Gehrman Sparrow didn't continue asking as he seemed to focus on his drinking. The bartender then added, "In the past two to three months, the Resistance has been very active. They've been constantly attempting to destroy railways or set up blockades on public roads. They've given the governor-general's office quite a headache, forcing it to dispatch large numbers of soldiers to maintain a smooth flow of traffic. However, the Resistance army seldom clashes with them head-on.

I'm aware of that. It's all going according to my plans... In a place where the natives and mixed-bloods take up 80%, it's not difficult for the Resistance to gather accurate intelligence. Coupled with external sponsors, it wouldn't be hard for them to live on for short periods of time. The reason why they were in a dire situation in the past was all because Kalvetua was an irrational sea serpent. It often got them to attack key nodes in a city, fighting head-on with the Loen Kingdom's navy and army... Klein mumbled to himself in delight as he drank another mouthful of beer.

He had also warned the Resistance to not be overly active. If they forced the governor-general's office into a corner, the Loen Kingdom might send Sequence 5 Beyonders or even demigods who were good at pursuit and tracking. With the Resistance's strength, there was no way they could put up a fight. In the name of Sea God, Klein's instructions were to maintain while slightly improving their present situation and await for the global situation to change.

This wasn't too far off. According to the various matters Miss Justice had reported, and with The Hanged Man giving insight that affirmed this, it was certain that once the Loen Kingdom was done with its internal reforms and made both its existing and newly commissioned ironclad warships its main force in combat, a war against the Southern Continent's colonies was inevitable.

He silently drank his beer until the bartender was done. He then put on his hat, got up, and left the bar, heading straight for his inn.

Along the way, he saw bronze-skinned children with curly hair and natives with brown jackets and pantaloons. These people either made way in horror without even looking up, or they were hunched in a corner, looking at Klein with complicated expressions in their eyes.

Klein helplessly curved the ends of his lips as he silently returned to his room.

He didn't immediately change his appearance and head to the various bars to seek out pirates. This was because he was certain that they would hide the entire night and not appear again.

Just as Klein was planning to enter Groselle's Travels to explore it, there was a knock on his door.

Without needing to ask, all he did was grip the knob when the visitor's appearance naturally surfaced in his mind.

It was a middle-aged man dressed in a dark red coat and white slacks. He wore a ship-shaped hat. The corners of his eyes and mouth, as well as his forehead, had clear wrinkles. He was none other than the captain of the White Agate, military personnel, Just Elland.

Impressive. News of me appearing in Bayam just spread, and he managed to find where I'm staying... Of course, it's because I didn't attempt to hide myself. I directly used my identification documents to check in... Klein turned the knob and pulled open the door. He politely greeted him, "Good evening."

"Good evening. I'm very glad to see you back in Bayam." Elland took off his hat and entered the room without any reservations.

"Is there something?" Klein pulled a chair over and sat down.

Elland sat opposite him and chuckled.

"Isn't visiting a friend the most important matter?"

You're such a smooth talker. Too bad Anderson is gone, or he can learn from you! Klein felt triggered for some reason.

He maintained his usual state and looked into Elland's eyes.

"Alright, you've made your visit."

Elland seemed to have expected such a response. He smiled and said, "Do you still remember Donna?"

Of course, she and her brother are adorable. I wonder if they've recovered from the fright I gave them the last time and have given up any curiosity towards the mysterious world... Klein calmly replied, "I haven't lost my memory."

"Forgetting unimportant matters is good for one's mental health. Heh heh, this wasn't said by Emperor Roselle." Elland simply explained, "Didn't you tell Donna's father that he should post an advertisement in the Sonia Morning Post for three days in a row, asking to buy Damir's special cured meat if he needs your help? He did so recently, but unfortunately, you weren't in Bayam or the nearby seas."

"What happened after that?" Klein's instincts told him that Donna and her family weren't in serious trouble based on Elland's relaxed attitude.

Elland chuckled and said, "I heard your conversation back then, and after discovering the advertisement, I visited them using the address. As you know, Donna is a lovely lady. She reminds me of my daughter.

"The matter isn't too complicated. Urdi, who's Donna's father, had a bunch of valuable goods plundered by the Crazy Captain. This plunged him into a terrible economic situation. Although it isn't as bad as going bankrupt, life for him has definitely turned for the worse. That's why he wishes to commission you to recover the goods back.

"I knew you weren't in Bayam back then, so I had to go through my own channels to help Urdi recoup the goods at a reasonable price."

"Crazy Captain?" Klein first found the title familiar before recalling where he had heard it before.

According to red-haired Helene, Vice Admiral Ailment Tracy's delivery of slaves for the Demoness Sect was passed off to Crazy Captain Connors Viktor. The latter appeared to have close connections with many of the slave dealers and merchants in the Loen Kingdom.

And this was tied to the truth behind the human disappearances in the colonies, as well as the underlying reason of the Great Smog of Backlund!

I've always been trying to investigate this matter. Back when I attacked Tracy, that was half the reason, but unfortunately, I didn't succeed. Later, I was busy acting for the digesting of the potion and my search for mermaids, so I temporarily abandoned my investigations... Klein's thoughts raced as he asked without a perturbed look, "Where's Crazy Captain now?"

"Urdi has already gotten back his goods," Elland said, emphasizing that the matter had been settled.

Klein looked at him and repeated, "Where's Connors Viktor now?"

Elland shook his head in exasperation.

"I've no idea, but his subordinates are still in Bayam trying to gather intelligence. As you know, Admiral Amyrius has returned to Backlund, and he's been replaced by Admiral Davis. Many changes are in place, so the pirates need to have a new grasp of the latest situation.

"Therefore, I guess that Connors's ship is docked in the shadows of one of the nearby islands, but that's a guess with no way to confirm."

After listening in silence with his hands clasped, Klein said, "Thank you."

Elland exhaled and looked out the window as his expression rapidly turned solemn.

"I came here today to tell you that you're too eye-catching. The higher-ups are beginning to pay attention to you. A powerhouse that can easily kill Kircheis wouldn't be ignored!

"It's best you stop using this identity for checking in; otherwise, you might face some difficult problems."

Klein nodded seriously and said, "Alright."

He was very grateful for Captain Elland's warning, but he didn't show it on his face.

After Elland left, Klein immediately changed his appearance, switched inns, and confirmed that he wasn't tracked by the military.

After doing all of that, he began considering the search for Crazy Captain Connors Viktor.

To others, this was an extremely difficult task, but Klein had a trick up his sleeve.

It was to use the Sea God Scepter and communicate with nearby sea creatures!

As long as Connors Viktor was in the Rorsted Archipelago's waters, there was no way he could hide from Klein!

Of course, it was necessary that he had enough time to perform the search.

Chapter 710: Plan

Above the gray fog, Klein extended his hand and summoned the Sea God Scepter from the junk pile. He began browsing through the countless points of light swirling about the Sealed Artifact.

Every point of light corresponded to a believer's prayer. The lights swirled with ethereal holiness!

Soon, Klein did a preliminary screening as he focused on the waters instead of the archipelago. After he had isolated the Sea God Scepter above the gray fog, he was unable to directly use the "divine artifact" to sense the surrounding waters and do anything effective. He had to rely on the scenes of his believers' prayers as a foundation before reaching out five nautical miles to influence the corresponding undersea creatures.

Klein emanated his spirituality and touched one of the specks of light. He saw a native performing his daily prayers in the evening on a fishing boat before docking.

As Klein's thoughts changed, the viewpoint of the scene quickly rose as more dark clouds and undulating waves appeared in the scene.

There are signs of a storm brewing hours ahead? That's right. I did find the atmosphere oppressive when I was out... That's why Elland said that Crazy Captain Connors's ship is likely to be hiding in the shadows of some island. It was to avoid the storm... Klein tapped the edge of the mottled table as he muttered silently.

This deduction made his "search" much easier. This was because he didn't need to search the extensive waters; all he needed to do was focus on the different islands around those waters.

With a change in thought, a blue gem at the tip of the Sea God Scepter in Klein's hand lit up.

On the waters away from the fishing boat, the crimson moonlight shone down on the deep blue waves that were slowly rising. Suddenly, splashes silently surged upwards as an exaggerated eye looking up into the sky surfaced. Beneath the eye was a massive silhouette.

At the same time, all kinds of fish began surfacing nearby.

They took in the indistinct halo that came scattering down, and they circled around before swimming down and headed in different directions.

Ten seconds later, the waters within a five-nautical-mile radius returned to their previous state. The deep blue waves swelled high again, awaiting the final eruption.

Phew... This is a little draining... Klein raised his left hand to rub his temple.

He wasn't merely commandeering the sea creatures; he had also bestowed a trace of Beyonder spirituality upon them. It allowed them to transmit the scenes they saw by praying after they discovered any docked ships around the island.

This wasn't too difficult or complicated for the Sea God Scepter, but it was burdensome and draining for the controller, Klein.

Then, Klein selected a few praying believers situated on different islands around the archipelago. He then used the same method to command the sea creatures of the region.

After doing all of this, he didn't bother throwing the Sea God Scepter into the junk pile before returning to the real world. He took off his coat and collapsed into bed.

Having expended his spirituality to the limit, he believed that he would fall asleep immediately. Yet, his head began aching, preventing him from opening his eyes or falling asleep.

Klein could clearly feel that his skin seemed overly sensitive. Lumps grew over his skin, and hidden beneath them were countless meat tendrils.

Indeed, as Mr. Hanged Man said, if you drain your spirituality to zero for two consecutive days, you'll definitely start hearing things and show signs of losing control. My body became a little abnormal just from reaching my limits once despite not even maintaining it for too long. Of course, it's because I've only recently advanced, and I haven't digested much. Furthermore, there's the excess from the potions from the prior Sequences... Klein regained his train of thought as he attempted to Cogitate the stacked spherical lights so as to calm the fatigue of his body and mind.

Having gradually recovered, he finally fell asleep. By the time he woke up, it was in the middle of the night.

At that moment, the winds were howling outside. The rain was pouring as the storm had finally unleashed its might after a prolonged period of brewing.

And this wasn't a rare situation in Bayam. Apart from that, the night was relatively serene.

Klein went to the washroom to clear his bowels. Washing his hands and taking four steps counterclockwise, he once again appeared above the gray fog.

He picked up the Sea God Scepter at the end of the long bronze table and began browsing through the "scenes" from the sea creatures.

The scenes showed ships, all located at different ports, harbors, or the shadows of different islands.

Although Klein hadn't seen Crazy Captain Connors Viktor, he had previously learned of his traits and the various emblems of his pirate crew. Therefore, he wasn't afraid of failing to identify him.

Scenes flashed past as he patiently scrutinized the details of the ships.

About ten minutes later, unconcealed joy appeared in his eyes. He zoomed in on a scene as he pulled the orientation of the scene closer.

He had found the suspected ship!

The ship was docked at the back of Symeem Island, an island furthest from the Rorsted Archipelago. Behind it was a towering cliff with churning waves below it.

Its sails and flag had been reefed up, but there was a white skull emblem wearing an eyepatch on both sides of the ship.

This was none other than the emblem of the Crazy Captain's pirate crew!

Klein removed the spirit pendulum from his left wrist and confirmed it through divination.

The answer made him rejoice, as it was indeed Connors Viktor's ship!

And Crazy Captain only had one ship!

It's no wonder that despite most adventurers at sea knowing that Crazy Captain is in contact with the kingdom's human traffickers and slave merchants, the Church of Storms's investigations of the colony disappearances didn't point to him. He only has a bounty of 3,300 pounds. The pirate crew only has one ship, so it just looks like it's a small business. It's impossible for him to do large-scale human trafficking... What should I do next? Klein first sighed silently before considering his angle of investigation.

His first reaction was to summon a massive undersea creature, hitch a ride on a whale-like creature, and head straight for Symeem Island while the storm continued. Then, he could infiltrate and use his powers as a Marionettist to control Crazy Captain Connors Viktor without causing too much of a stir. But on careful thought, he felt that it was a rash decision.

It wasn't a problem dealing with other pirates who weren't at the pirate admiral level, but since Crazy Captain Connors Viktor was involved with a terrifying matter like the Great Smog of Backlund, he had to consider that Connors's normal actions were just a disguise. In consideration of how he might have another identity, with the ship concealing some secrets, there was a high chance that there was a trap that could deal with pirate admirals. Klein might not walk out alive despite being armed to the teeth if he made such a rash infiltration attempt.

Should I summon myself and head over with the Sea God Scepter as a Spirit Body? This can avoid some accidents, as I can immediately end the summoning if anything goes wrong. However, the Sea God Scepter is essentially the Beyonder characteristic of a High-Sequence Beyonder from the Storm pathway. As long as it appears in the real world, it might attract Sea King Jahn Kottman... This will prevent the situation from developing as I expect it to... Klein looked at the storm in the scene as he came up with a preliminary plan.

Before that, he conjured a pen and paper and wrote the divination statement: "Dealing with Connors Viktor is very dangerous."

Picking up the silver chain of the spirit pendulum, Klein reined in his thoughts and focused on the divination.

Soon, he opened his eyes to see the topaz pendant turning clockwise at a high frequency and a wide arc.

This meant that dealing with Connors Viktor was very dangerous!

It's as I expected. Thankfully, I didn't rashly rush over... Klein picked up the Sea God Scepter and decided to execute the plan he had just thought of.

It was to use the prayer scene and strike Connors Viktor's ship with the Sea God Scepter from afar by using the cover of the storm. He would startle the snake in the grass and force out its secret!

After grasping the relevant situation, he would end the attacks before Sea King Jahn Kottman sensed the abnormality or arrived at the scene.

Klein wouldn't feel disappointed if Connors Viktor unfortunately landed in the hands of the Sea King. This was because he would have to think of the means to pass the information to the three Churches after he figured out the truth of the Great Smog of Backlund.

If Crazy Captain successfully escaped, Klein would've uncovered his secrets by startling the snake in the grass. He could then subsequently formulate a plan of attack!

Phew... Klein slowly exhaled as he watched the ship bob about with the dark crashing waves before raising the Sea God Scepter high in the air.

At the tip of the scepter, the blue gems that circled around it had begun to emit a bright light.

Chapter 711: The Storm Attacks

Symeem Island. Beneath the towering cliff, the dark seawater churned violently under the waves surging in from all directions.

Aboard Crazy Captain Connors Viktor's ship, the Single-eyed Skull, several pirates donned cloaks and pulled up their hoods. Heavy rain pattered against them as they struggled against winds strong enough to lift a child, making their way out of the cabin to inspect the ship and prevent any accidents.

Their cloaks were made of linen, but the surfaces had been coated with a sticky liquid that had already hardened. The rain couldn't penetrate that layer and could only run down onto the deck.

The liquid was Donningsman tree sap, produced in the rainforests of the Southern Continent. It was naturally water-resistant and quite common. It had originally been rather cheap, but after a research team suggested last year that it might promote hair growth, its price had skyrocketed.

"Weather like this is perfect for staying inside the Red Theater, drinking liquor, smoking weed, and holding women in your arms!" A pirate looked over the side of the ship and grumbled.

His companion pulled at the hood of his cloak and echoed, "I heard a batch of newcomers arrived at the Red Theater. I really want to give them a try."

"How did you hear about that?" another pirate casually asked.

He was met with a chuckle.

"I heard it from the boss. It's not like you don't know what the Captain's 'business' is. That's why the boss knows plenty of human traffickers. Ha! They prefer to call themselves 'slave merchants.'"

"Speaking of that, I remember what happened that time." The first pirate wore a reminiscent expression. "Among the 'goods' sent over was a young noble lady who had run away from home. Her skin, figure, and looks, tsk--they were... they were... I don't even know how to describe them. I still remember her to this day. What a pity she committed suicide!"

As they spoke, their vision suddenly brightened, and they subconsciously looked up at the sky. Apart from the pattering rain, an abnormal bolt of silver lightning was snaking through the dark clouds that concealed the crimson moon and stars.

Suddenly, a gigantic bolt of lightning that illuminated the surrounding waters struck down, heading straight for the Single-eyed Skull!

Boom!

Lightning spread out wildly as the wooden ship burst into flames. Deafening thunder reverberated in the pirates' ears.

Immediately afterward, silver bolts of lightning struck down like brandished claws. The tiny sailboat was instantly plunged into a forest of lightning.

At that moment, the thick bolts that were about to interweave suddenly separated. Defying the laws of nature, they failed to strike the Single-eyed Skull. Instead, they hung close around the ship before striking the pitch-black water, illuminating the surrounding sea as sizzling arcs of electricity snaked outward in a spectacular display.

The pirates on deck still suffered from the initial strike. One had been charred black like an overburned piece of wood, while two others collapsed, their bodies convulsing.

There really is a problem!

Above the gray fog, Klein couldn't help but sigh when he saw his Lightning Storm being dispelled.

He was certain that this was a power at the level of a demigod!

If he had rashly infiltrated the Single-eyed Skull, even with Creeping Hunger, Death Knell, and Groselle's Travels, there was no way he could have put up any effective resistance against such bizarre powers. By then, he wouldn't even have had time to pray to himself and respond with the Sea God Scepter from above the gray fog!

Taking a deep breath, Klein made all the blue gems at the tip of the bone-white scepter light up.

Around the Single-eyed Skull, two kinds of sounds emerged amid the storm. One was piercingly shrill, as though it could puncture one's eardrums and penetrate one's brain. The other was deep and heavy, resembling the beating of a heart as it struck one's Spirit Body.

This left the pirates in extreme discomfort, and many felt the urge to vomit blood. However, this was only the beginning. The splashing of water intensified as a dark wave surged to a height of nearly ten meters, rising opposite the island's cliff!

The wave resembled a wall created by a deity, pushed toward the Single-eyed Skull by "His" invisible hand.

This was a tsunami created by Klein!

It could already be considered a calamity!

The roar of the wave sounded like a series of explosions. The pirates aboard the ship stared at the dark sky, the churning clouds, and the massive tsunami. It felt as though the apocalypse described in myths and legends had arrived. They lost all will to save themselves.

But as they waited in despair for the final judgment, a disturbance that defied logic and scientific law appeared in the middle of the tsunami. An indescribable vortex rapidly took shape and tore through the entire wave, causing the terrifying wall of water to swiftly collapse!

Amid a thunderous rumble, a secondary wave hurled the Single-eyed Skull high into the air as the previous gigantic wave dispersed, dumping part of its load onto the ship. One of the masts snapped, the ship was left in shambles, and even the deck was completely flooded.

Whoosh!

A fierce wind suddenly stirred and swept up the pirates. It transformed into a hurricane that exceeded its natural limits, pushing the airborne Single-eyed Skull farther out to sea.

The ship rode the squall and flew through the sky, covering several nautical miles without landing in the undulating waters. It was as though it had become an airship steadily proceeding forward.

Klein was astonished that the demigod or corresponding Sealed Artifact aboard the Single-eyed Skull had nullified the tsunami. He lamented his low Sequence, for even after stirring the powers of the mysterious space above the gray fog, he still couldn't create a tsunami as destructive as the one Kalvetua had produced. At the same time, he controlled the hurricane and ensured that the Single-eyed Skull didn't lose its support and plummet, afraid that it would leave his five-nautical-mile range of influence.

At that moment, his goal wasn't to destroy the Single-eyed Skull or capture Crazy Captain Connors Viktor, but to force the demigod or Sealed Artifact hidden aboard the ship to reveal itself.

A powerhouse or item of this level was rare in the world. As long as he saw its appearance, he would sooner or later learn who or what it was, as well as which organization it belonged to!

That would provide a direction for his subsequent investigation into the Great Smog of Backlund!

I hope it isn't from the Seer pathway. Otherwise, who knows whether the face I see will be the real one... However, that would also be a good thing. Since I'm investigating the truth behind the Great Smog, I'll uncover what Ince Zangwill is doing and prepare for my eventual revenge. I'll also be able to successfully find the Bizarro Sorcerer potion formula and main ingredients... As Klein sighed inwardly, he made a gem at the tip of the Sea God Scepter emit a pure azure light.

With a hum, the Single-eyed Skull's flight suddenly changed. It plummeted like a stone without even managing to glide!

Then, it abruptly became extremely light and landed on the surface of the sea like a feather brushing against a person's face.

At that moment, Klein, who had expended a great deal of spirituality, was about to muster his remaining strength to create another tsunami when he heard an explosive boom from within the scene.

It was a terrifying sonic boom!

Furthermore, it differed from an ordinary sonic boom, as though the howling of the wind had been mixed into it.

It came from Sea King Jahn Kottman! Although he was still some distance away, he held dominion over these waters, allowing him to exert his influence across space while rushing over!

This was a Sequence 3 Saint, an existence close to the level of an angel!

The terrifying sonic boom sent the Single-eyed Skull flying. There had been no hesitation over whether it would harm anyone. After all, there was nothing wrong with striking down pirates!

At the same time, Klein sensed a terrifyingly powerful consciousness sweep across the area, searching for anything abnormal. It affected the scene before his eyes, causing everything to turn indistinct.

After withstanding another blast, Klein calmly and rationally ended the response. He closed the corresponding prayer scene and tossed the Sea God Scepter into the junk pile.

The difference isn't trivial. If Sea King hadn't appeared, I wouldn't have been confident in forcing out that demigod. My Sequence is still too low, making the scepter overly burdensome and troublesome to use... However, the Storm pathway really is powerful at sea. They're practically mobile calamities.

The powers displayed by that demigod contained the key elements of distortion, disorder, magnification, and the violation of normalcy. They appear to be demigod-level powers from the Black Emperor pathway. Others might not recognize them, but I possess the corresponding Card of Blasphemy! It definitely isn't merely the power of a Sequence 5 Mentor of Disorder... An Earl of the Fallen? Hmm, the military possesses part of the Black Emperor pathway's potion formulas, but they seem limited to the first five Sequences. They lack Sequence 4 and above...

Although Klein had failed to force the demigod or corresponding Sealed Artifact to reveal itself, its reactions had provided him with several clues. He suspected that a particular faction within the royal family wasn't only cooperating with the Demoness Sect and Ince Zangwill, but was also secretly in cahoots with a faction that controlled most of the Black Emperor pathway's Sequences.

The descendants of Solomon or Trunsoest? Is the King of the Five Seas, Nast, involved in this? Klein was deep in thought when he heard overlapping prayers by his ear.

His train of thought was interrupted. He instinctively extended his spirituality and touched the rippling light beside his chair.

Then, he saw Danitz.

The well-known pirate had his eyes closed as he piously prayed to The Fool.

...

Klein blankly took out his pocket watch and checked the time several times, confirming that it was nearly three in the morning.

Is he insane? Why is he praying in the middle of the night? Does he not want me to sleep? Klein watched carefully in exasperated amusement and discovered that Danitz appeared tipsy. Faint singing could even be heard outside.

The crew of the Golden Dream is having another bonfire party? Are they celebrating Gehrman Sparrow's departure? To think they celebrated all the way into the middle of the night! Klein instantly understood why Danitz was praying at that moment.

He took a deep breath and projected his will into the halo, saying in a deep voice, "Those who say my name are on my mind."

Sea King Jahn Kottman arrived at Symeem Island and began searching for the source of the abnormal tsunami, as well as the ship belonging to an unknown pirate crew.

He was certain that there had been a demigod aboard that ship!

The tall, brawny cardinal of the Church of Storms, whose thick hair was dark blue, was also a high-ranking deacon of the Mandated Punishers. He clenched his fists, and the dark clouds in the sky split open, allowing crimson sunlight to spill across the sea.

After identifying the positions of the stars, Jahn Kottman flew in a particular direction.

Suddenly, he slowed down because he saw a pirate ship flying a flag emblazoned with the Single-eyed Skull.

There was no one manning the ship as it drifted with the wind. Scarlet flames burned everywhere, surrounded by charred corpses.

A middle-aged man was tied to the middle of one of the masts. He wore a triangular hat and a black eyepatch. His exposed eye was wide open and filled with horror. A wooden pole had impaled his chest, dyeing everything around it with blood.

His life and Spirit Body had completely dissipated.

Chapter 712: The Loyal Alger

Beyond the storm, crimson moonlight shone through the thin clouds above the Golden Dream and entered one of its dark, unlit rooms.

Danitz stood motionless beside his bed. It was as though he had encountered a Beyonder power resembling Medusa's petrifying gaze.

He couldn't help grinding his teeth as his feet trembled imperceptibly. His mind was filled with endless grayish-white fog and an indistinct figure standing high above everything, uttering the awe-inspiring words, "Those who say my name are on my mind."

Th-there really was a response... There really was a response! Danitz's lips quivered as he muttered inwardly. He felt his calves go weak.

This was the first time he had ever received a response to a prayer!

He had been frightened out of his wits!

He had long known that The Fool was an unknown existence worshiped by the secret organization backing Gehrman Sparrow, and that he had already established a connection with "Him" by chanting "His" honorific name. If he acted disloyally or betrayed "Him," he might immediately die an inexplicable death. However, this understanding came only from the knowledge Vice Admiral Iceberg Edwina had taught him. He had never encountered a similar situation before, nor had he ever imagined that an unknown existence would respond to him.

When the fog, figure, and voice suddenly appeared before his eyes and ears, he learned for the first time that mighty existences would directly respond to their believers!

Yes, deep down, Danitz had unknowingly changed his description of "Him" from an unknown existence to a mighty existence.

As soon as he recovered from his shock, he hurriedly took several deep breaths and attempted to pace around to relieve the lingering terror in his heart. However, just as he took a step with his right foot, he discovered that his legs had gone limp. All he could do was collapse onto his bed before barely managing to turn around and sit up.

"It really is a mighty existence. It's real..." Danitz muttered softly as he clearly realized that he had become involved in something serious.

Back in the book world, he had merely chanted the honorific name without noticing anything abnormal, so all he had felt was fear born from knowing the possible consequences. Now, he was facing a hidden danger that had finally revealed its outline, as well as a future he couldn't see clearly. How could he not plunge into an inescapable abyss of extreme fear?

After an unknown period, Danitz exhaled and consoled himself.

It might not be a bad thing. At the very least, Gehrman Sparrow is still alive, and he's living quite well!

With that thought, he forced a smile.

I'm a member of a secret organization, and in the future, I'll be someone blessed by a mighty existence...

As his thoughts raced, Danitz decided to pray every morning after waking. He believed that no existence would dislike a pious believer.

Of course, he would keep the revelation in mind and often pray silently.

The next morning, Klein woke at his usual time despite having exhausted himself the previous night.

He slowly got out of bed and saw that the sky was blue while the ground outside was wet. The entire world appeared to have been washed clean by water, making it exceptionally fresh. However, the scattered leaves, broken branches, and all kinds of trash indicated that the night had been anything but peaceful.

After washing up, Klein put on an ordinary Loenese face and ordered a cup of Gurney Sap, a drink native to Symeem Island, and a rather substantial Teativa for breakfast to make up for his expenditure the previous night.

As he drank the beverage resembling lemonade mixed with sugar and milk, he ate the fresh, fragrant dish that combined mutton and fish with the sweet and slightly sour flavor of fruit. In high spirits, Klein picked up the newspapers provided by the inn and began reading, starting with the Sonia Morning Post and News Report.

Near the end of breakfast, he opened Strange Cases, a newspaper rather popular among adventurers, and saw a striking headline:

"Bloody Infighting Amid Storm:

"According to sources, intense infighting occurred aboard Crazy Captain Connors Viktor's Single-eyed Skull. The pirates executed Crazy Captain and killed one another. Reports indicate that there were no survivors.

"All this violence was concealed by last night's terrifying storm. No one knew of the conflict until the Single-eyed Skull was found drifting near Symeem Harbor."

The article included a blurry photograph that appeared to have been secretly taken from the harbor.

The Single-eyed Skull's distinctive features stood out in the photograph. It was badly damaged, with many areas charred black. Only one mast remained intact, while a figure wearing a triangular hat had been pinned to the middle mast.

It's Connors Viktor... He died just like that? Klein's pupils contracted as he contemplated the matter seriously. It can almost be confirmed that there was a demigod aboard the ship last night... When he realized that Crazy Captain had been targeted, or when Sea King began pursuing him, he could only look after himself. Unable to take Connors with him, he decisively silenced him and destroyed all the evidence?

Klein, who had planned to continue pursuing Crazy Captain, felt dejected. He realized that although the trail of clues hadn't been completely severed, very little remained.

The only thing he knew at present was that the demigod most likely belonged to the Black Emperor pathway!

Judging by the intensity of last night's storm, the Single-eyed Skull was likely sent to the harbor by Sea King Jahn Kottman for a follow-up investigation. I wonder if they will discover anything else... Yes, I can have Mr. Hanged Man keep an eye on the matter within the Church of Storms... There's no need to inform him now, since the Tarot Gathering is this afternoon. The World can directly commission him then... Klein quickly made up his mind and drained the rest of his Gurney Sap.

He then returned to his room and planned to retrieve the radio transceiver he had stored above the gray fog for an extended period to contact Arrodes. He wanted to see if it had any other clues regarding the Bizarro Sorcerer potion formula.

Having left the waters east of Oravi, where an Aurora Order demigod had previously targeted him, Klein now dared to use items tainted with the gray fog's aura. However, he knew very well that he couldn't do so frequently or for too long each time. Otherwise, there was a possibility that the True Creator would detect it.

Because of this, as well as his wariness of Arrodes, he planned to do whatever he could himself and seek advice from others whenever possible. He would only play the question-and-answer game when he had no other options.

Aboard the Blue Avenger, which was docked at the Resistance's private harbor.

Alger planned to make one final resupply before returning to Pasu Island.

After instructing his crew on which items to purchase, he changed into local clothing and headed straight for Bayam. After circling around several times, he arrived at the Cathedral of Waves, planning to report recent developments to the diocesan bishop, Chogo.

Although he was returning to Pasu Island to report his work to the Church's upper echelons, he knew very well who his immediate superior was. He needed to behave appropriately and avoid making his superior believe that he was bypassing the chain of command to establish direct connections with the upper echelons.

Chogo remained hale and hearty. Pleased with Alger's proactive report, he said in a brisk tone after listening, "There's no need to worry. It's merely a routine report. I've already informed His Eminence Kottman that you are devoted to the Lord and loyal to the Church. You are one of our most trusted captains, and His Eminence Kottman will inform the Council of Cardinals of this."

He paused, but without giving Alger time to speak, he continued, "In addition, there's another mission. Investigate those closely connected to Crazy Captain Connors Viktor.

"This mission was issued directly by His Eminence Kottman. You must take it seriously."

Investigate people connected to Crazy Captain? Alger was puzzled, but he didn't question the order. Instead, he struck his right fist against his left breast and said, "Yes, Your Excellency."

The grizzled Chogo nodded. After thinking for two seconds, he asked, "Do you know Gehrman Sparrow?"

The question came like a bolt from the blue. Alger's pupils contracted, and he nearly lost control on the spot. Fortunately, his mental fortitude allowed him to barely maintain his composure.

"I've heard of him. He's become very famous recently. Not only did he hunt Wormtongue Mithor and seriously injure Tracy, but he also boarded Cattleya's Future." Alger spoke in a flat tone to conceal the turmoil in his heart.

Chogo gave a terse acknowledgment.

"You were at sea, so you're a little behind on the news.

"Gehrman Sparrow killed Kircheis in Toscarter last week and collected his bounty. Heh heh, that pirate really was a Devil, a Sequence 5 Devil."

"Kircheis? Agalito's second mate?" Alger asked with genuine emotion.

He knew that Kircheis was suspected of being a Sequence 5, but he hadn't known that he belonged to the Devil pathway. And the fact that he was both a Sequence 5 and a Devil implied many things. It meant he was extremely difficult to kill, which in turn meant that Gehrman Sparrow had very likely killed Kircheis during a chance encounter!

This meant that Gehrman Sparrow was already at the pinnacle of Sequence 5!

If it weren't for the fact that he's seeking to purchase a Sequence 4 potion formula, I might even suspect that he has become a demigod... Alger thought, feeling perturbed.

He was alarmed to realize that in a single week, The World had obtained the Sequence 5 Ocean Songster potion formula and its main ingredient, killed a Sequence 5 Devil, and quite possibly acquired a Sequence 6 Notary potion formula as well!

How did he do it? Alger realized that he was beginning to feel a little afraid of The World.

Of course, it wasn't something he found impossible to accept. This was because he knew that The World was Mr. Fool's Blessed, and Mr. Fool clearly had more than one Blessed. If one was responsible for the Notary potion formula while another handled the Ocean Songster matter, then it was possible that Gehrman Sparrow had only killed Kircheis.

Although that was similarly alarming, it no longer seemed like an invented tale.

Chogo nodded solemnly.

"Yes. Agalito's lack of a reaction implies something.

"Focus on gathering information about Gehrman Sparrow."

"All right, Your Excellency." Alger bowed reverently, having already decided to be as perfunctory as possible with this mission.

Inside the inn, Klein placed the radio transceiver on the table.

Before long, a rapid series of clacking sounds rang out.

Chapter 713: Three Questions

Amid the clacking sounds, the radio transceiver spewed out another sheet of illusory paper. Written on it in Loenese were the words: "Great Master, your loyal and humble servant, Arrodes, has finally caught up with you!"

...There's no need to be so excited... Oh, Arrodes speaks as professionally as ever. It didn't complain bitterly about being unable to contact me for so long, nor did it ask why I hadn't sought it out. Instead, it blamed itself for failing to catch up with me... It's making me feel a little guilty. Still, I need to remain wary... Klein was momentarily at a loss as to how to answer.

Without waiting, Arrodes used the radio transceiver to produce an emoticon of someone cautiously peeking out on the illusory paper.

"Great Master, ruler above the spirit world, your servant senses that you have taken another step toward returning to your holy throne. Am I right?"

This fellow has evolved really quickly. It can already use colored text to create a rudimentary emoticon... From Arrodes's perspective, I'm a true god gradually rediscovering myself? Is that why, even though it can clearly sense that I'm only a Sequence 5, it still treats me with such respect and acts even more humbly? Klein understood that the mirror had deliberately asked him a question, so he nodded frankly.

"Yes."

"You have already answered my question. In exchange, and in accordance with the rule I must abide by, you may ask me one question." Arrodes rapidly "typed" its response before adding a "smiley face."

Without hesitation, Klein asked directly, "Where can the Bizarro Sorcerer potion formula be found?"

The illusory paper spat out a long string of complicated symbols before turning into a mirror that displayed a lifelike scene.

It was a dark cathedral devoid of natural light. Inside were a number of squirming shapes, all of them terribly blurry. They looked like a pencil drawing that had been rubbed with an eraser, making it impossible to discern any precise details.

However, Arrodes added a block of text beneath the scene.

"This is Zaratul. While advancing to Sequence 1, Attendant of Mysteries, 'He' lost control and became a monster. However, Great Master, you must be careful. 'He' is a very crafty person. Perhaps everything about 'Him' is merely an act serving some purpose.

"I'm unable to look directly at 'Him,' as doing so would harm me. Apart from 'Him,' you will be unable to obtain the potion formula from any demigod of the Secret Order. Back then, Zaratul directly provided them with the High-Sequence potions, and there is almost no way to reverse-divine the formula through Beyonder means."

What a detailed answer. It even provided the additional information that Sequence 1 of the Seer pathway is Attendant of Mysteries... Does that mean an angel who attends upon Mysteries? From the looks of it, the Secret Order route will only work if I face Zaratul directly and obtain the potion formula from "Him." And I'm not even capable of looking directly at "Him"... No wonder the Snake of Fate, Will Auceptin, told me to seek out the mad Zaratul without mentioning the Secret Order... Klein was moved by Arrodes's attitude. If he didn't believe that his level and strength were insufficient to control this Sealed Artifact, he might truly have considered treating it as his servant.

Amid the clear clacking sounds, another section of illusory paper appeared, presenting a different scene.

It showed a towering mountain peak crowned by a swath of dilapidated palaces. Within them stood a gigantic stone chair.

Klein was very familiar with this scene. Even without Arrodes's annotation, he knew what it represented.

The Antigonus family's treasure hidden on the main peak of the Hornacis mountain range!

White paper continued to spill out as a new scene appeared. Like a movie, its perspective shifted.

The first image to enter Klein's eyes was a lofty Gothic bell tower surrounded by beautiful palaces.

The former was the Bell of Order, and the latter was Sodela Palace. They were landmark buildings in Backlund.

The scene changed, and soon a new building appeared on the paper. It was a pitch-black cathedral with two symmetrical bell towers.

The view of the cathedral's interior expanded until the whole nave was visible, then settled on a pair of iron-black doors that swung open.

The doors were abnormally heavy. Seven Dark Sacred Emblems adorned them, as though standing guard over the Tenebrous Heaven.

Chanis Gate... Saint Samuel Cathedral... Klein recognized the familiar style of the doors. From the architecture, he confirmed that the cathedral was the headquarters of the Church of the Evernight Goddess's Backlund diocese--Saint Samuel Cathedral!

More white paper emerged, and the scene changed as it plunged deep into the darkness. An ancient notebook rested upon an empty bookshelf made of bone. It was a black, hardbound book.

Klein recognized the notebook at once.

It was the Antigonus family's notebook that had caused the original Klein's death!

After going in circles, everything had come full circle!

Klein silently observed it for a moment. Only after the scenes disappeared did he regain control of his thoughts.

Yes, back then, the Aurora Order members were able to see the Clown potion formula in the notebook. Since it has acknowledged me, the contents it reveals to me will definitely be completely different from what I saw when I leafed through it before. It should contain the Bizarro Sorcerer potion formula. All I would lack are the ingredients or characteristics.

So this notebook has been sealed behind Saint Samuel Cathedral's Chanis Gate all this time. Taking it from there won't be any easier than finding Zaratul and facing "Him" directly... High-Sequence Beyonders were involved in the Great Smog of Backlund. After I reported the matter to the Church's Backlund diocese through Miss Justice, the situation was quickly brought under control. This is sufficient proof that the Backlund diocese possesses considerable power. They certainly don't lack demigods or Sealed Artifacts... Yes, no matter what, I first need to return to Backlund and see whether any opportunities arise. By comparison, I'd rather not head to the Hornacis mountain range...

Reining in his thoughts, Klein looked at the radio transceiver, which had turned dark and solemn.

"Who was the demigod aboard Crazy Captain Connors Viktor's ship last night?"

Clacking sounds promptly rang out. The previous illusory paper vanished, and new sheets were spat out.

The content on the paper was likewise a lifelike scene.

Five candles of varying heights cast warm light from an exquisite brass chandelier. A middle-aged man wearing a triangular hat and a black eye patch stood before a cabinet filled with wine, champagne, and tequila. He was gazing humbly at the person opposite him.

Across from him stood a tall figure in a black cloak, its face completely concealed beneath its hood.

The figure didn't seem to have a real head. There was only a mass of severely distorted darkness embedded above its neck.

Thanks to the portraits on the bounty notices, Klein recognized the one-eyed man as Crazy Captain Connors. His disheveled, greasy hair hung down and happened to cover his neck.

The one opposite him is probably that demigod, but he deliberately disguised himself and made the appropriate anti-divination preparations. The fact that Arrodes can produce a scene of this quality is already impressive enough... Klein wasn't too disappointed. Instead, he carefully memorized the figure's build.

More than 1.85 meters tall, but less than 1.9... Rather long arms. When his hands hang at his sides, they almost reach his knees... Broad shoulders holding up the cloak... His feet point outward to a certain degree...

As an expert in disguise, Klein believed that when someone had disguised themselves and prepared a certain degree of anti-divination measures, there was a high chance they wouldn't think to conceal their build, especially if their physique had no particularly distinctive traits.

Therefore, this could provide a few clues. It might allow Klein to recognize the person on sight!

"Very good. It's your turn to ask." After committing those details to memory, Klein stopped scrutinizing the figure and waited with interest for Arrodes's question.

He was curious to see how Arrodes would continue to overturn his impression of it.

The typing slowed, as though it were hesitating. Bit by bit, the illusory white paper emerged.

"Great Master, c-can I say something to you?"

"Yes," Klein answered, puzzled. He began looking forward to what Arrodes had to say.

The typing hastened, exuding an obvious warmth.

A single line appeared on the illusory paper: "Great Master, happy birthday!

"This is belated. Your present body was born on March 4th, 1327. I originally wanted to wish you a happy birthday at midnight that day, but I failed to catch up with you."

...This really is a topic beyond my expectations... I even forgot my own birthday... The corners of Klein's lips twitched as he found himself at a loss for words.

He had inherited the original Klein's fragmented memories and some of his emotions. He knew the date of his birthday, but why would someone who led such a solitary life remember something like that?

This fellow is actually the first person to wish me a happy birthday... Benson and Melissa must be feeling especially upset today... The interview should have ended in February. I wonder if Benson succeeded in becoming a civil servant... Klein felt a pang of melancholy, and his gaze toward the radio transceiver grew warmer.

After a moment's thought, he calmly asked, "Third question: What are your origins?"

The typing paused for two seconds before sounding again.

A sheet of white paper emerged, and a new scene surfaced.

Large quantities of black, viscous liquid gushed from the ground, spreading outward in a distorted fashion. The liquid sprouted varying numbers of arms and legs, transforming into strange monsters that charged forward.

During this process, a speck of light shot out with the black liquid and landed on a rock. It rapidly fused with the stone, transforming it into a mirror with ancient patterns and black gemstones adorning both sides.

What kind of bizarre scene is this... Is this how Arrodes was born? What was that speck of light? Where did it come from? It does seem like a Beyonder characteristic... Klein made a preliminary interpretation of the scene.

The typing didn't stop, and another line appeared: "Great Master, do you have any more questions?"

Noting the time, Klein shook his head.

"No."

"You have finished asking your questions, and it is time for me to leave. Great Master, ruler above the spirit world, your loyal and humble servant Arrodes looks forward to serving you again and hopes to continue following in your footsteps. Bye~" At the end of the illusory paper was a waving-hand emoticon.

Chapter 714: New Diary Pages

Inside the inn's quiet room, the dark and silent radio transceiver instantly returned to normal, and the eeriness surrounding it vanished.

Klein hurriedly set up a ritual and sacrificed it above the gray fog before taking the time to consider the answers he had received.

Combining the clues provided by Arrodes with the answers from Mr. Azik and Will Auceptin, Klein now had a basic idea of how he should proceed.

He didn't merely wish to return to Backlund for some rest and reorganization. He also viewed the metropolis as his main base of operations for a considerable period. He wanted to see whether he could steal the Antigonus family's notebook from Saint Samuel Cathedral of the Church of the Evernight Goddess.

If he confirmed that there was no feasible solution, he would attempt to switch to a neighboring Beyonder pathway. If that proved impossible as well, he would have no choice but to take the final route--heading to the main peak of the Hornacis mountain range.

Hmm... I definitely can't use my identity as Sherlock Moriarty after returning to Backlund, unless I want to draw out Ince Zangwill or the royal family faction hiding in the shadows. However, when contacting people I know or making use of the Church of Steam's resources, I can transform back into Sherlock Moriarty.

In short, I have to create a new identity. Furthermore, it must be entirely separate from Gehrman Sparrow. Hmm, I won't return directly to Pritz Harbor this time. I'll make a detour through Desi Bay and ensure that no one can trace my origins. Klein's thoughts raced, and he soon formed a clear plan.

After determining his next course of action, he planned to draw some blood and head above the gray fog to explore Groselle's Travels with his Spirit Body. However, considering that there was a Tarot Gathering that afternoon, he lay back on the bed and took the opportunity to rest and relax.

The Future sailed smoothly across the undulating waves as Admiral of Stars Cattleya stood by the window, gazing at the seemingly endless scenery. From the deck came Nina's exasperated shouts from time to time as she angrily berated Frank Lee for creating a batch of fast-growing, beef-flavored mushrooms that liked to eat fish.

Cattleya sighed silently and pushed the heavy glasses up the bridge of her nose.

At that moment, her spiritual perception was suddenly triggered, and she looked down at her desk. At some unknown point, several sheets of yellowed paper had appeared there.

The emperor's diary entries... She finally sent a messenger... Delighted, Cattleya turned around and picked up the few sheets of paper that seemed akin to a preliminary probe.

By three in the afternoon, after memorizing the symbols, Cattleya saw illusory crimson light surge toward her like a tide, drowning her within it.

Beneath a high dome supported by stone columns, Cattleya saw similar crimson beams rise and transform into indistinct figures.

She didn't examine them carefully, because she realized that a familiar pair of glasses had naturally appeared on the bridge of her nose. They were an almost perfect replica of the pair she wore in the outside world.

Then, from the corner of her eye, she saw Miss Justice elegantly rise to her feet. Miss Justice briskly greeted the figure seated at the place of honor, "Good afternoon, Mr. Fool~"

Audrey had been in a good mood recently. After returning to her family's castle, she had played the part of an obedient daughter and helped her mother, Lady Caitlyn, resolve several psychological problems stemming from her age and physical condition. The feedback had been exceptionally good, allowing Audrey to summarize more of the Psychiatrist acting principles.

The most important among them was to help others resolve their psychological problems. She suspected that this was why the potion was named Psychiatrist rather than Psychologist.

Miss Justice is very happy... Her mood proved infectious, and Klein responded with a smiling nod.

During this process, he noticed that Mr. Hanged Man appeared preoccupied. He seemed hesitant and conflicted, as though he hadn't yet made up his mind.

Eh, this means Mr. Hanged Man can afford the Ocean Songster potion formula and main ingredient, but he'll have to pay an enormous price. I thought he would ask to pay in installments... The Fool Klein calmly withdrew his gaze without betraying any unusual emotion and surveyed the area.

After Miss Justice finished her greeting, Cattleya looked toward the head of the long bronze table.

"Honorable Mr. Fool, I have found three pages of Roselle's diary."

Here they are... I wonder whether the three diary pages specially selected by Queen Mystic will contain anything valuable. For instance, they might provide clues to the Seer pathway's Card of Blasphemy. That would give me another alternative... Filled with anticipation, Klein calmly and naturally said, "Very good. You may think of a request."

"Yes, Mr. Fool." Cattleya replied impeccably before requesting permission to conjure the contents of the diary pages.

Seeing her behavior and recalling how she had acted in the dream, Klein felt rather gratified. He felt like a teacher who had successfully reformed a student.

This is the first time Ma'am Hermit has provided pages from Emperor Roselle's diary. Given the standards and abilities she has displayed, it's impossible that she failed to find any in the past... Yes, she was more of an observer before. After being punished by Mr. Fool, has she truly integrated herself into our Tarot Club? Coupled with Cattleya's performance last week, Audrey reached a conclusion.

Meanwhile, she sensed that Mr. Hanged Man didn't seem like himself. He appeared to be looking forward to something, yet also seemed unable to bring himself to part with something. She found this rather intriguing.

Klein didn't have to wait long before the three diary pages were conjured and appeared in his hands.

Derrick and the other members instinctively fell silent so as not to disturb Mr. Fool's reading.

"February 9th. Today is Bornova's birthday. He is already a healthy, strong, honest, and kind young man. His piety has earned the Church's favor.

"Everyone is congratulating me, saying that Bornova will definitely become a Blessed, a saint. They praise him for the purity and flawlessness of his faith.

"I should be very happy, but I can't conceal my dismay. I wish for my children to be more human, to possess more emotions. I hope they can be themselves rather than merely believers of a deity.

"However, almost everyone sincerely believes that Bornova's present state is excellent. Matilda is the same, as is Ciel. Most of my noble officials feel the same way. Only Bernadette shares my view. She told me privately that she believes people should be more self-centered, so long as they don't harm others.

"Recalling what Zaratul said when Bornova was born, as well as the premonition I experienced after becoming a demigod, my heart grows heavier. Adorable angel. Heh heh, what an adorable angel.

"Is this your way of guarding against me?

"Is this your attempt to control me?

"No, you can probably never understand the pride within me. As far as I'm concerned, so what if you're a deity? I can replace you!"

"February 11th. Another attempt to contact Zaratul ended without success.

"Ever since he obtained that Antigonus notebook, the leader of the Secret Order has appeared less and less often. I have no idea what secret he is plotting. In any case, 'He' definitely won't tell me.

"February 12th. I made a mystical item particularly suitable for Bernadette.

"It can effectively shield her from the Hidden Sage's ravings, as long as she doesn't proactively pray and seek a response. Yes, this item also seems capable of helping Saints enter the easternmost reaches of the Sonia Sea, where the battlefield of the gods lies. It prevents them from being influenced by the True Creator's ravings, and that place likely conceals the secret of the Forsaken Land of the Gods.

"Ha, I was merely making a present for my daughter on a whim. To think it would possess such powerful effects. Bernadette, your father truly is the greatest Artisan in the world!"

Greatest Artisan? Does this mean the emperor had already reached Sequence 2 of the Savant pathway when he wrote this diary entry, becoming an angel? With the God of Steam and Machinery occupying the pathway, this is the highest level an Artisan can attain... No wonder Queen Mystic can freely enter and leave the battlefield of the gods. It's because of a gift from the emperor. Indeed, a child with parents is like a treasure... Klein felt wistful.

In addition, he could sense Emperor Roselle's repressed anger in the diary entry. Using the reading comprehension skills honed in his previous life, he discerned what lay between the lines.

After he became an angel, the Church of the God of Steam and Machinery obstructed and suppressed him. The God of Steam and Machinery had exerted a certain influence over his youngest son, Bornova, turning him into the most devout of believers and designating him as someone who could become an angel and enter the Land of Perfection.

Not only did this force Emperor Roselle to submit to the suppression out of fear for the consequences, but it also stirred rebellious thoughts within him.

Did the Emperor later become a public enemy of the world because of this rebellion? Klein nodded inwardly.

Meanwhile, he noticed something regarding Zaratul. He suspected that the Miracle Invoker had begun seeking advancement to Sequence 1 only after obtaining the Antigonus family's notebook. To that end, "He" had made numerous plans, but something had gone wrong during "His" advancement, turning "Him" into a monster. Of course, as Arrodes had said, Zaratul might not have lost control. "He" could be concealing something or plotting something.

After quickly scanning the rest, Klein turned the page and looked at the reverse side. It wasn't a continuation, but entirely new content.

"October 5th. Several believers of the Primordial Moon were captured by the Men in Black organization I established. They failed to complete the corresponding sacrificial ritual.

"Their behavior intrigues me, as their power seems to come directly from the crimson moon.

"Unfortunately, a vast amount of data has confirmed that the crimson moon is truly a satellite orbiting our planet. Then how does it combine physics and mysticism?

"With my present strength, traveling to the crimson moon isn't impossible. At most, it would be troublesome and somewhat difficult, but it doesn't seem necessary."

"October 17th. I have finally made up my mind.

"I will attempt to switch to the neighboring Mystery Pryer pathway. Only then can I become a Sequence 1.

"I've always suspected that the Hidden Sage is merely a conceptualized Uniqueness that gained sentience and awakened because of some accident. Therefore, this pathway's Sequence 1 position is likely still vacant!"

Chapter 715: Madness in His Later Years

"I've long since obtained the Knowledge Emperor potion formula from that most ancient organization. Now, it's time to seek the corresponding ingredients. It might already belong to a certain angel, or it might have fused with a nearby object and turned into a twisted, evil monster or a chaotic and terrifying Sealed Artifact. In short, I must be careful. It would be best to find a suitable helper.

"Knowledge Emperor. The name of this Sequence 1 potion is rather interesting. If I hadn't seen the Blasphemy Slate with my own eyes, I would've imagined that it belonged to the Reader pathway and, therefore, to the Church of the God of Knowledge and Wisdom. Stranger still, these two pathways aren't interchangeable.

"I once discussed this matter with that mysterious leader and Old Mister Hermes. We basically reached a consensus. We believe that the Reader pathway represents the 'omniscient' aspect of 'omnipotent and omniscient,' while the Mystery Pryer and Savant pathways correspond to knowledge itself, divided into two aspects: one leans more toward the mystical, while the other leans more toward reality.

"Heh, once I become a Knowledge Emperor, Bernadette will no longer have to worry about the Hidden Sage's knowledge infusion. Nor will she have to fear pursuing knowledge."

The Emperor eventually switched to the Mystery Pryer pathway? I wonder if he successfully became a Knowledge Emperor... Perhaps it was because of this that his relationship with the Church of Steam completely fractured, leaving him a public enemy with no one to help him. All he could do was place his hopes on the Twilight Hermit Order... Klein thought with mixed emotions as he formed several conjectures.

Apart from that, he was rather interested in the Emperor's explanation of why the Reader pathway wasn't interchangeable with the Mystery Pryer and Savant pathways. He believed that, by deepening his understanding of the matter, he might be able to discern the rules and rationale behind the interchangeability of Beyonder pathways.

To the average Beyonder, the Reader, Mystery Pryer, and Savant pathways all belong to the domain of "knowledge." There should be a very strong connection among them, yet the former is different...

Reader belongs to the "omniscient" aspect of "omnipotent and omniscient." Then does Secrets Suppliant, the Shepherd pathway, represent the "omnipotent" aspect? There must be a certain overlap between the two. For example, one can become powerful through erudition and erudite through power. Therefore, they can be interchanged...

Following this line of thought, the Storm pathway can be understood as omnipotence in the domains of sea, land, and air. Spectator corresponds to the psyche, making it a powerful complement to Storm. Then why can the Sun pathway be interchanged with them? Considering that the ancient sun god was deemed "omnipotent and omniscient," does this mean the Sun pathway is the foundation that accommodates "omnipotence and omniscience"?

Yes... Following this line of reasoning, Mystery Pryer and Savant are different aspects of "knowledge" itself. That's why they can be interchanged. Then how are Assassin and Hunter distinguished? What about the Evernight, Death, and God of Combat pathways? In an instant, Klein thought of many things, but he lacked the time and sufficient knowledge to delve deeper into his analysis. All he could do was temporarily put the corresponding questions to the back of his mind and will the third diary page to the top of the stack.

After a cursory glance, Klein's attention suddenly sharpened. This was because the diary page was very different from the previous ones.

It bore no date, and the words were widely spaced. Since this was the original, it was obvious that the force exerted while writing the words had been far from ordinary!

Klein swept his gaze across the page, and its contents were reflected in his mind.

"No! Impossible!

"How could that happen!?

"If my predictions are correct, I'm not the only one who had that encounter!

"No. No! How could that happen!?

"What I saw tells me that everything will be destroyed. Everything I've created as well! No! I can't accept such an ending!

"I have to do everything I can to save myself. I can't rely on the seven deities!

"Only by ascending to the throne of Sequence 0 can I and the things I value be preserved!

"Should I attempt to pull Mr. Door back into the real world? No! Although 'He' claims to be only a Sequence 1, I believe 'He' isn't merely an ordinary Sequence 1! It's very likely that 'He' will bring an unforeseen catastrophe upon me!"

The Chinese characters, far larger than those on the first two pages, occupied the yellowed paper in a haphazard manner. There wasn't much content, but it left Klein's head throbbing painfully.

When he wrote this diary entry, Emperor Roselle was very likely already a Sequence 1 Knowledge Emperor. The content he wrote was infused with a potent sense of mystery and colored by his emotions. This also meant that, had Klein read it in the real world, he might very well have become mentally unstable, gone mad, or lost control on the spot!

Thankfully, Ma'am Hermit doesn't know Chinese. Otherwise, she would've mutated while memorizing the contents... Even without understanding Chinese, she must've found the process exceptionally tiring and draining... Prolonged exposure would inevitably produce visual and auditory hallucinations. Furthermore, there's a high chance that the early signs of losing control would appear... Klein thought, privately congratulating himself on his good fortune.

His attention quickly returned to the diary entry as puzzlement surfaced within him.

What did the Emperor see that made him so emotional? His state of mind was also rather extreme.

No, the agitation and rashness revealed in these words are a little abnormal. They don't match the image of someone who has occupied a lofty position for a long time. They don't match an angel's level. Even in his final days, he said that after his death, he didn't care if a flood deluged the heavens. He didn't lose his composure or appear this agitated then.

Who influenced him? Or did something corrupt and taint his thoughts?

Also, he mentioned that he wasn't the only one who had that encounter. What was he referring to? Transmigration? It's true that he isn't the only one who transmigrated for no apparent reason. There's also me... or are there even more?

As thoughts flashed through his mind, Klein didn't waste any time. He made the three diary pages vanish, then smiled at Cattleya.

"Have you decided on your request?"

Cattleya bowed slightly and said, "I wish to know why Emperor Roselle went mad in his later years."

Audrey, who sat in the same row as her, blinked. She thought she had misheard, for she hadn't expected Ma'am Hermit to immediately ask such a weighty question.

Furthermore, how does she know that Emperor Roselle went mad in his later years? Also... Also, she only handed in three pages. How can she ask a question of this magnitude? It doesn't conform to the principle of equivalent exchange! Many thoughts arose in Audrey's mind, but she didn't interrupt Ma'am Hermit's question. Instead, she waited for Mr. Fool's response with keen interest and inexplicable excitement.

Alger, Emlyn, and Fors similarly held their breaths and cast their gazes toward the end of the bronze table. Only Derrick and The World seemed oblivious.

Klein considered the question and chuckled.

"First, we need to accept reality.

"I'm neither omniscient nor omnipotent."

He said those words in a relaxed, self-deprecating manner, but The Hermit and the others showed no signs of unease. Instead, they appeared even more reverent.

To them, it was obvious that Mr. Fool wasn't omnipotent and omniscient. Setting aside their guesses regarding Mr. Fool's state, this hidden existence's honorific name contained no corresponding descriptions, which was enough to explain the matter.

Furthermore, none of the present true gods or evil gods had honorific names involving omnipotence and omniscience!

After setting their expectations, Klein began answering The Hermit's question.

"I also wish to know what Roselle encountered in his later years.
